*Compiled 6 November 2026 by three sub-agents of Local (CM-1a, b, c) from the 680 cards of Wave 1, updated from Waves 2 to 4. Sub-agent self-checks: a verdict here changes no status in the record. Verdicts follow the design's §5: a theorem closes only its stated class, a measurement shuts nothing, a failed search closes nothing, and a new hand closure is pending until a worker reads it. The compilers worked before GPT's GC2690, so doors on Mortality, AM-3's amplification, CT-2's check sums and SEP show PENDING where [GPT's carrying review](GPT-SW02-CARRY-REVIEW-20261106.md) has since carried a repaired statement: read that review first. The three parts merged within themselves, and aliases across parts are noted, not merged. The coverage is a finite manifest, not all possible methods.*

## ALIAS

- **tock partners** (target: Amplification, DQ-006). A forgotten free tock gives a same-width partner survivor. *Scope:* Partner exists iff the flip is forgotten, a finite computation; equal width; no finite edit shortens. A partner y2 has G^s(y2)=G^s(y), so it is a sibling in the fixed-target guarded inverse tree, which AM-2 records as closed for amplification. ALIAS of AM-2's closure. Leaf-width comparison applies to any sibling count: a partner is a sibling at the same width, and 2^d leaves live at width W - 2d, not W. *Source:* reports/rule30-sideways-finite-bridge.md:58; DEEP-QUESTIONS.md:88 *Evidence:* ['AM-1.1: CANDIDATE; SKM VALID WITH REPAIR; SKR PARTLY KNOWN', 'AM-3.1: CANDIDATE; SKM VALID WITH REPAIR; SKR PARTLY KNOWN', 'flag 21: PARTIAL', 'flag 136: CONF…

- **Gate trap and Q01Gap** (target: CQ01Empty). Right half enters the 829-window trap, so gaps are 3 or 5; Q01Gap excludes finite Psi. | Attack Psi empty as one deterministic sequence. *Scope:* Trap-entering R only. Nothing excludes period 2. One door under two names (the gate-trap reduction and CQ01Empty attacked as one sequence); it is R-02's door with CP-1, compiled by CM-1a. Populations kept apart: fixed R = empty, and trap-entering R; no Q01Gap-to-all-CQ01 arrow. Evidence noted so nothing is lost. BN-16 had not reported when this ledger was compiled; finite repetition there would be a candidate only, without a closed-state certificate. *Source:* Rule30GateTrap.gaps_of_entry, u_empty_gaps, CQ01Empty_of_Q01Gap; INSTINCT.md:1683 *Evidence:* ['CP-1.1: INCONCLUSIVE', 'CP-1.2: CANDIDATE', 'CP-1.3: UNJUSTIFIED; rescued', 'CP-2.2: CANDIDATE; SKM VALID WITH REPAIR; SKR PARTLY KNOWN', 'CP-2.3: CANDIDATE;…
